async function inputCenterRunAutoOCR(drafts){
  const targets=[];`nfor(const d of drafts){d.ocrStatus='OCR처리중';`nd.ocrError='';`nconst pages=Array.isArray(d.pages)&&d.pages.length?d.pages:[{filePath:d.filePath}];`npages.forEach((p,i)=>{if(p.filePath)targets.push({key:`${d.draftId}::${i}`,path:p.filePath});`n});`n}
  render('inputs');`nif(!targets.length)return;`nconst result=await window.schoolBoard?.runInputOCR?.({files:targets});`nif(!result?.ok){drafts.forEach(d=>{d.ocrStatus='OCR오류';`nd.ocrError=result?.reason||'로컬 OCR 호출 실패';`n});`nrender('inputs');`nreturn;`n}
  const byKey=new Map((result.results||[]).map(x=>[x.key,x]));`nfor(const d of drafts){const pages=Array.isArray(d.pages)&&d.pages.length?d.pages:[{filePath:d.filePath}];`nconst texts=[],engines=new Set(),errors=[];`npages.forEach((p,i)=>{const r=byKey.get(`${d.draftId}::${i}`);`nif(!r)return;`nif(r.ok&&r.text)texts.push(`[${i+1}페이지]\n${r.text}`);`nif(r.engine)engines.add(r.engine);`nif(!r.ok)errors.push(`${i+1}p ${r.reason||r.status||'OCR 실패'}`);`n});`nd.text=texts.join('\n\n').trim();`nd.rawText=d.text;`nd.ocrEngine=[...engines].join(' + ');`nd.ocrError=errors.join(' / ');`nd.ocrStatus=errors.length?(d.text?'OCR확인필요':'OCR오류'):(d.text?'OCR완료':'OCR확인필요');`ninputCenterApplyOcrMatch(d);`n}
  render('inputs');`ntoast(`로컬 OCR 완료 · 자동매칭 ${drafts.filter(x=>x.studentNo).length}건 · 확인필요 ${drafts.filter(x=>!x.studentNo||/오류|확인필요/.test(String(x.ocrStatus||''))).length}건`);`n}

    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")

    // 하위 헤더 별칭으로 안전하게 찾습니다.
